# A2 Vocabulary List (Wortschatz)

A curated collection of authentic, level-appropriate German vocabulary for the **A2 (Elementary / Grundlegende Kenntnisse)** level according to the CEFR standards. Each entry includes the word with grammatical markers, English definition, topic category, **5 to 6 varied example sentences utilizing der/die/das and non-repeated pronouns**, and direct reference links.

[📖 A2 Grammatik-Leitfaden](./Grammatik.md) | [📌 A2 Kurs-Übersicht](./README.md) | [🏠 Hauptmenü](../README.md)

---
